Full working site scanned against 20 existing theme fundamentals.
Every atom has a verified evidence line in the log and a block in the evidence index.
This map names what a person meets and which theme kind matches — not repairs.
Atoms: 12188 · Hits: 564

## How matching was decided
- Lens: `_theme-fundamentals.md` object_kind + failure_kind per theme.
- Universe: 12,188 atoms across 39 served pages, 2 iframe documents, shared chrome once.
- Hit rule: both object_kind and failure_kind must match the atom's visible control or prose.
- One serial agent read each source_file at source_lines and logged verbatim evidence_snippet.
- Density gate passed before scan; evidence verification required snippet in source.

## theme-01 — Hero headline and Explore banks button are not centered in the top block

Fundamental: These are the same themes as the theme files, boiled to the general problem. They are not a new grouping and not a fix list.

Hits: 2 (original: 2, new: 0)

Shroffin is here to advance banking itself. If shroffs were one stage in how banking evolved here, Shroffin is built for the next: banking that is more transparent, more informed, and more firmly aligned with the customer. Hits: 23 (original: 23, new: 0)
